During the contract the applicable rate will be\ncalculated i.t.o. fee adjustment specification.\nGeneral: Method of Payment\nPayment under item 3.7.1(a) – (g) will be made as follows:\nThe units of measurement shall be the lump sum.\nThe rate under each sub-sub-item (i) shall include full compensation for all duties and requirements associated\nwith the administration and monitoring of the Works Contract as specified in Clauses C3.1.4, C3.7.2 and\nC3.7.4. It shall further include for all costs, disbursements, overheads and profit but excluding the costs,\ndisbursement and profit related to the transport and subsistence of the Engineer and Design Specialist(s).\nC3-83\n\nFee adjustment under each sub-sub-item (ii) shall only be applicable when the accepted tendered amount or\nfinal Cost of the Works differs by more or less than 20% of the Employer’s estimated Cost of the Works. The\nfee adjustment shall be determined as described in Clause C3.1.4.\nThe Service Provider shall be entitled to render interim monthly accounts, based on the proportion of the Cost\nof the Works completed.\nThe unit of measurement for pay item 3.7.1(h) shall be the month (pro rata for part of a month).\nThe tendered rate shall include full compensation for the transport and subsistence of the Engineer and Design\nSpecialist(s) as well as all related costs, disbursements and profit.\nThe unit of measurement of pay item 3.7.1(i) and (j) shall be the month (pro-rata for part of a month). The rate\ntendered shall include full compensation for all duties and requirements associated with the administration and\nmonitoring of the Works Contract as specified in Clauses C3.1.4, C3.7.2 and C3.7.4 during an extended Works\nContract duration or prolonged site stoppages not under the control of the Employer. It shall further include for\nall costs, disbursements, overheads and profit but excluding the costs, disbursements and profit related to the\ntransport and subsistence of the Engineer and Design Specialist(s) during an extended Works Contract\nduration or prolonged site stoppages not under the control of the Employer.\nThe provisional sum for pay item 3.7.1(k)(i) shall include full compensation for the remuneration of the Assistant\nContract Engineer but shall be limited to 25% (twenty five percent) of Total Annual Cost of Employment of the\nindividual approved as assistant.\nThe prime cost sum under pay item 3.7.1(k)(ii) is to cover the disbursement cost of the Assistant Contract\nEngineer for attending monthly meetings.\nContract price adjustment shall be applicable in accordance with Clause C2.1.6.\nItem Unit\n3.7.2 Occupational Health & Safety obligations\n(a) Continuous compliance and monthly internal month\naudits\n(b) External audits provisional sum (PS)\n(c) Handling costs i.r.o. sub-item 3.7.2(b) percentage (%)\nThe unit of measurement under pay item 3.7.2(a) shall be the month (pro rata for part of a month).\nThe tendered rate shall include full compensation for all costs, including personnel, transport, accommodation\nand subsistence of the Occupational Health and Safety Specialist for fulfilling the Service Provider’s obligations\nas the Employer’s agent with respect to the requirements of the Construction Regulations of the Occupational\nHealth and Safety Act (Act 85 of 1993).\nContract price adjustment shall be applicable in accordance with clause C2.1.6.\nThe services required under 3.7.2(b) shall be undertaken by an independent auditor whom the Service\nProvider shall procure and be approved by the Employer. The provisional sum shall cover all the independent\nauditor’s costs for travel, accommodation and fulfilment of the service (including reports).\nThe minimum requirement for qualification and experience for the OHS Specialist are specified in Clause\nC3.1.11.\nThe provisional sum shall be paid for in accordance with Clause C2.1.8.\nThe percentage tendered for handling costs under 3.7.2(c) shall include for all costs associated with the\nplanning, scheduling, compilation of quotations, issuing of 3 (three) quotations in electronic and/or paper\nformat and evaluation for the procurement of the sub-service, as well as handling fees.\nPayment under these pay items may only be claimed from the date of official hand-over of the Works to the\nContractor up to and including 1 month after the issue of the Taking-Over Certificate.\nContract price adjustment shall be applicable to 3.7.2(a) in accordance with Clause C2.1.6.\nC3-84\n\nItem Unit\n3.7.3 Establishment of supervisory personnel on site\n(a) Relocation costs of individual site personnel prime cost (PC)\n(b) Handling i.r.o. pay item 3.7.3(a) percentage (%)\n(c) Establishment of office equipment lump sum (LS)\nThe unit of measurement for pay item 3.7.3(a) shall be the prime cost sum.\nPayment under this item shall be made for all costs related to establishing the individual supervisory personnel\non site and their removal after completion of the Works, as approved by the Employer.\nThe prime cost shall be paid for in accordance with Clause C2.1.8.\nThe unit of measurement for pay item 3.7.3(b) shall be the percentage.\nPayment under this pay item shall be made on the amount actually spent and shall include full compensation\nfor the handling costs of the Service Provider.\nThe unit of measurement for sub item 3.7.3(c) shall be the lump sum.\nThe sum tendered shall be for establishing and de-establishing of all office equipment on site and shall include\nfor all associated costs. Payment shall only be made once all office equipment has been established on site.\nContract price adjustment shall be applicable to sub-item 3.7.3(c) in accordance with Clause C2.1.6.\nItem Unit\n3.7.4 Provision of Supervisory Staff and Equipment\n(a) Site supervision staff\n(b) Mark-up i.r.o. sub item 3.7.4(a) provisional sum (PS)\n(c) Surveyor and assistants percentage (%)\n(d) Handling costs i.r.o. sub item 3.7.4(c) prime cost (PC)\n(e) Provision and monthly cost of office equipment percentage (%)\n(f) Project Liaison Officer (PLO) month\n(g) Mark-up costs i.r.o. sub item 3.7.4(f) provisional sum (PS)\n(h) Accommodation for supervisory staff percentage (%)\n(i) Mark-up costs i.r.o. sub item 3.7.4(h) provisional sum (PS)\npercentage (%)\nThe sum under pay item 3.7.4(a) is to cover the monthly (pro rata for part of a month) Total Annual Cost of\nEmployment (TACE) (including a 1.44 site staff overhead factor) of the supervision staff (excluding surveyor\nand his assistants, trainee (student) and Project Liaison Officer (PLO)) as listed in Clause C.3.7.4 and\napproved by the Employer.\nPayment under this pay item shall only be applicable for the period actually established on site and shall in no\ninstance be prior to the date of official hand-over of the Works to the contractor or after 28 days of the date of\nthe Taking-Over Certificate for the Works contract. The TACE shall be verified by means of an audited\nstatement.\nThe unit of measurement for pay item 3.7.4(b) shall be the percentage.\nThe percentage tendered shall include full compensation for all additional overhead costs related to the\nemployment of the relevant personnel as specified in Clause C3.7.4(a).\nThe unit of measurement for pay item 3.7.4(c) shall be the prime cost sum.\nPayment under this item shall cover for all costs related to establishing a surveyor, survey assistants and all\nequipment on site and their removal after completion of the Works, as approved by the Employer. It shall\nfurther include for the full survey services including the accommodation. Transport for this service shall be paid\nunder item 3.7.6.\nC3-85\n\nThe unit of measurement for pay item 3.7.4 (d) shall be the percentage.\nThe percentage tendered shall include for all administrative costs and handling fees.\nThe unit of measurement for pay item 3.7.4(e) shall be the month (pro rata for part of a month).\nThe rate tendered for office equipment shall include full compensation for supplying and maintaining all office\nequipment as specified in Clause C3.7.4(d), including all incidentals, all monthly charges such as cell phone\nand mobile data costs and insurance to operate the equipment.\nContract price adjustment shall be applicable in accordance with Clause C2.1.6.\nThe provisional sum under pay item 3.7.4(f) is to cover the Total Annual Cost of Employment (TACE) of a PLO\non site as specified in Clause C3.7.7.\nThe unit of measurement for pay item 3.7.4(g) shall be the percentage.\nThe percentage tendered shall include for all costs associated with the sourcing by advertisement, employment\nand mentoring of the PLO.\nThe provisional sum under pay item 3.7.4(h) is to cover the cost of accommodation for supervisory staff.\nThe unit of measurement for pay item 3.7.4(i) shall be the percentage.\nThe percentage tendered shall include for all costs associated with the reimbursement by the Service Provider\nfor rented accommodation, as well as handling fees and profit.\nThe provisional and prime cost sum items shall be paid for in accordance with Clause C2.1.8.\nContract price adjustment shall be applicable to sub-item 3.7.4(e) in accordance with Clause C2.1.6.\nItem Unit\n3.7.5 Training\n(a) Employer’s candidate engineers person month\n(b) Students (engineering or surveying) receiving person month\nexperiential training\n(c) Student stipend provisional sum (PS)\nThe units of measurement for sub-item 3.7.5(a) and (b) shall be the person month.\nThe rates tendered shall include full compensation for the Service Provider’s costs to provide training to the\ndifferent categories of trainee inclusive of all costs to communicate with the Employer and any other body or\norganisation in respect of work assigned to the trainees as specified in Clause C3.1.20. The rate tendered\nshall also include telephone calls and charges, stationery and information technology hardware, software,\nconnection or licence costs and for lost production, profits and all other incidentals as well as all administrative\nand overhead costs.\nThe provisional sum allowed under pay item 3.7.5(c) is to cover the monthly stipends as prescribed by the\nEmployer to be paid to the students. Where circumstances justify need to pay for the accommodation of\nstudents, this shall be discussed with the Employers project manager.\nThe provisional sum item shall be paid in accordance with Clause C2.1.8.\nPro-rata payments shall be made for partial months for training provided based on a 23 working day month.\nContract price adjustment shall be applicable to sub-items 3.7.5(a) and (b) in accordance with Clause C2.1.6.\nItem Unit\n3.7.6 Transport for site supervisory staff and for\nadditional services\nC3-86\n\n(a) Travelling to perform duties provisional sum (PS)\n(b) Mark-up i.r.o. item 3.7.6(a) percentage (%)\n(c) Toll fees prime cost sum (PC)\nThe provisional sum is to cover the cost of travelling to perform the duties as specified in Clause C3.7.5 and\nany additional duties as may be ordered by the Employer. The rate for transport in excess of 3000km ) per\nvehicle per calendar month shall, however, be reduced and paid for at 60% of the rate. Completed log sheets\nfor each vehicle shall be certified by the Engineer and included in the monthly payment certificate.\nThe unit of measurement for pay item 3.7.6(b) shall be the percentage.\nThe percentage tendered shall include for all administration, handling cost and profit incurred by the Service\nProvider.\nThe provisional sum item shall be paid in accordance with Clause C2.1.8.\nThe prime cost sum is to cover the cost of toll fees for site supervisory staff performing their duties.\nPayment will only be made for E-tag transaction upon proof of an official statement via the Employer’s\nTransaction Cleaning House (TCH), which is the integrated fee collection and information management\nprocess for Electronic Tag Collection (ETC) accounts.\nItem Unit\n3.7.7 Assistance at Clarification Meeting, Tender Period\nand Tender Evaluation of sub-contract packages\n(a) Service Provider’s cost number\n(b) Tender Evaluation number\n(c) Evaluation Report number\nThe unit of measurement for sub-item 3.7.7(a) shall be the number of clarification meetings held. The rate\ntendered shall include for all cost incurred by the Service Provider with respect to the duties for assisting the\ncontractor at the clarification meeting and tender period as specified.\nThe unit of measurement for sub-item 3.7.7(b) shall be the number of sub-contract/work packages evaluated\nby an independent person. The rate tendered shall include full compensation for time related costs associated\nwith the evaluation of each sub-contract/work package and the compilation of a Tender Report.\nThe unit of measurement for sub-item 3.7.7(c) shall be the number of evaluation reports compiled by an\nindependent person. The rate tendered shall include full compensation for all disbursements, material, printing\nand other costs associated with the evaluation and compilation of the Tender Evaluation Reports.\nContract price adjustment shall be applicable in accordance with Clause C2.1.6.\nItem Unit\n3.7.8 Environmental Services during Construction Phase\n(a) Environmental services prime cost (PC)\n(b) Cost for procuring and managing sub-service lump sum (LS)\nprovider under sub-item 3.7.8(a)\nThe cost under sub-item 3.7.8(b) shall include all costs associated with the planning, scheduling, compilation\nof quotation/tender documentation, issuing of up to 10 (ten) quotation/tender documents in electronic and/or\npaper format and evaluation for the procurement and management of the sub-service including administrative\ncost and handling fees.\nThe prime cost item shall be paid in accordance with Clause C2.1.8.\nContract price adjustment shall be applicable to sub-item 3.7.8(b) in accordance with Clause C2.1.6.\nC3-87\n\nC3.8 ADDITIONAL DUTIES, SPECIAL SERVICES AND SPECIALIST ADVICE\nC3.8.1 Scope\nThis section covers additional work, other special services and specialist advice, reporting and other duties,\nincluding a dispute process, etc. that the Service Provider may be required to undertake over and above the\nnormal duties and obligations as specified. It also covers the cost of structured engagement with Community\nStakeholders and the Project Liaison Committee (PLC), including the development and ongoing mainte-\nnance/updating of a Targeted Enterprise and Targeted Labour Database.\nC3.8.2 Additional Duties, Special Services and Specialist Advice\na) By the Service Provider\nThe Employer may order additional duties, special services and specialist advice that fall outside the specified\nscope. Such additional duties, special services and specialist advice may involve, but not be limited to:\n• Additional design requirements\n• Evaluation of alternative tenders as specified in Clause C3.6.5\n• Additional investigations during the Defects Notification Period\n• Special services and specialist advice as specified in Clause C3.3.6\n• Diverse other services, etc.\n• Establishment and liaison with PLC during Design and Construction Phases\n• Establishment and maintenance of databases\n• Disputes (including disputes between contractor and sub-contractors)\nAllowance is made in the Pricing Schedule for payment on a time basis for any such work that may be required.\nThe level of expertise necessary for any such work shall be concomitant with the issues to be addressed.\nAny additional duties, special services and specialist advice shall be fully scheduled and submitted to the\nEmployer for approval prior to the commencement therewith.\nb) By the Employer\nAdditional duties performed by the Employer may involve, but not be limited to:\n• re-evaluation of any replacement personnel by the Tender Evaluation Panel\n• reviewing of draft documentation submitted more than once\nAllowance is made in the Pricing Schedule for payment on a time basis for any such service performed more\nthan once.\nC3.8.3 Payment and Monthly Reporting\nWhen submitting interim certificates for payment, the Service Provider shall use the Employer’s standard forms\nand formats. No payment can be made before the Service Provider is registered as a vendor on the Employer’s\nsystem.\nThe Service Provider shall submit payment certificates for all work rendered in the Employer’s financial year\nwithin the specific year.\nThe Service Provider shall submit and update on a monthly basis a cash flow forecast for the remuneration of\nthe full service to be rendered.\nThe Service Provider shall use the ITIS platform and modules to perform certain duties and to provide required\ninformation as listed in C3.1.21.\nThis includes using the Employer’s different ITIS platforms:\n• ITIS Web\n• ITIS Desktop\n• ITIS Mobile\nThe current modules applicable to this contract (modules can be running on any of the above platforms) and\ntheir description are as follows:\n(i) Contract Module – management of contracts\n(ii) Project Information Module – uploading of employment and training data\nC3-88\n\n(iii) Structures Module – uploading of structures details\nThe Service Provider shall use the South African Road Design System (SARDS) platform and modules to\nperform certain duties and to provide required information. The South African Road Design System provides\na comprehensive framework to facilitate the process of pavement design. The process is initiated with the\nregistration of projects at the road authority for tender purpose. Projects listed for tender are open to view by\naccredited users and these projects are populated with available network level data. The SARDS portal\nprovides convenient viewing capability for design engineers to gain insight into the design requirements of the\nproject.\nOnce appointed for the design of a specific project, the design engineer is supported with an array of tools\nthrough the SARDS portal that incorporates the latest pavement engineering technology.\n(i) Design Investigation A data management system that allows for capturing, viewing and processing of\nvast volumes of data associated with the design investigation process. Data views are presented in a\nlinear viewer format that is easy to navigate. State-of-the-art data analysis procedures including:\n• Classification of visual condition data using the deduct point system\n• Comprehensive deflection bowl analysis\n• Dynamic homogenous section demarcation using automated change-point detection\n• Point-by-point back-calculation of effective layer stiffness moduli using the latest multi-layer and\nnumerical optimisation routines\nA materials information system that:\n• Allows for capturing materials test results from existing pavement layers as well as potential\nexternal material sources including borrow-pits and commercial sources.\n• Prepares the necessary materials related input for the performance simulation component of the\nSARDS.\n(ii) Performance Simulation - Combined simulation of structural and functional pavement deterioration using\nmechanistic-empirical techniques. This also include Economic assessment of different pavement design\nalternatives with consideration of road authority and road user cost.\n(iii) Construction Quality Assurance\n• Quality assurance material test units based on TMH1 and the latest SANS 3001 test methods\n• Quality assurance adjudication schemes based on COLTO 8200 and 8300\n• Automated quality assurance and as-built reporting\nAllowance has been made for these requirements in the Pricing Schedule under payment item 3.8.5: Payment\nof Monthly Reporting Costs. Failure to fully comply with the duties as listed above may result in payments\nbeing withheld and/or termination in terms of Clause 8.4 of the Conditions of Contract.\nC3.8.4 Measurement and payment\nItem Unit\n3.8.1 Additional duties by the Service Provider\n(a) Personnel cost\n(i) Category A\n(ii) Category B\n(iii) Category C hour\n(iv) Category D hour\n(b) Disbursements hour\n(c) Handling cost i.r.o. sub-item 3.8.1(b) hour\nprime cost (PC)\npercentage (%)\nThe unit of measurement for sub-item 3.8.1(a) shall be the hour.\nRates have been provided for the different categories. The hourly rate provided under the different categories\nis deemed to include full compensation for all work related to the provision of additional duties extra-over the\nnormal duties as specified and as ordered by the Employer.\nContract price adjustment shall be applicable in accordance with Clause C2.1.6.\nC3-89\n\nThe prime cost sum allowed under sub-item 3.8.1(b) is to cover the disbursement cost of personnel\naccommodation, travel and subsistence associated with the undertaking of additional duties. Transport by\nprivate vehicle, if required, shall be paid at the rates under Section C3.7.\nThe Prime Cost shall be paid in accordance with Clause C2.1.8.\nThe unit of measurement for pay item 3.8.1(c) shall be the percentage.\nThe percentage tendered shall include for all administrative costs and handling fees.\nContract price adjustment shall be applicable to sub-items 3.8.1(a) and (c) in accordance with Clause C2.1.6.\nItem Unit\n3.8.2 Additional Duties by the Employer hour\nThe unit of measurement shall be the hour of Employer personnel utilised for additional duties.\nThe provided negative rate shall be for carrying out additional duties as specified in Clauses C3.1.11, C3.5.3\nand C3.8.2(b).\nThe minimum time will always be 2 (two) hours per key person re-evaluated and actual hours will be charged\nfor reviewing draft documentation submitted more than once. The value shall be deducted from the Service\nProvider’s interim monthly account immediately after having been informed by the Employer.\nContract price adjustment shall be applicable in accordance with Clause C2.1.6.\nItem Unit\n3.8.3 Special Services and Specialist Advice\n(a) Service or advice provided by Service Provider\n(i) Category A\n(ii) Category B\niv) Category C hour\nv) Category D hour\n(b) Service or advice procured by Service Provider hour\n(c) Handling costs i.r.o. sub-item 3.8.3(b) hour\nprime cost (PC)\npercentage (%)\nThe unit of measurement for pay item 3.8.3(a) shall be hour.\nRates have been provided for the different categories. The hourly rate provided under the different categories\nis deemed to include full compensation for all work related to the provision of the special service or specialist\nadvice as specified in Clause C3.3.7.\nContract price adjustment shall be applicable in accordance with Clause C2.1.6.\nThe prime cost sum shall be paid in accordance with Clause C2.1.8.\nThe unit of measurement for pay item 3.8.3(c) shall be the percentage.\nThe rate tendered shall include full compensation for all costs associated with the planning, scheduling, com-\npilation of quotation/tender documentation, issuing of up to 10 (ten) quotation/tender documents in electronic\nformat and evaluation for the procurement and management of the sub-service including administrative cost\nand handling fees.\nContract price adjustment shall be applicable to sub-item 3.8.3(a) in accordance with Clause C2.1.6.\nItem Unit\n3.8.4 Training of Targeted Enterprise Understudy provisional sum (PS)\nC3-90\n\nThe provisional sum for pay item 3.8.4 shall include full compensation for the Service Provider’s cost of training\nof Targeted Enterprises as understudy to the Contract Engineer (CE) including for all personnel and other\ncosts, disbursements, overheads and profit.\nThe provisional sum shall be paid in accordance with Clause C2.1.8.\nItem Unit\n3.8.5 Payment and Monthly Reporting cost month\nThe unit of measurement shall be the month.\nThe rate tendered shall include full compensation for registering on the Employer’s ITIS Project Information\nModule and for the management of the ITIS Software Modules (Contract, Information and Structures Modules)\nas specified in Clause C3.1.21. It shall also include full compensation for capturing and submitting the required\ninformation regarding training, empowerment, capacity building, targeted enterprise development, labour and\nstaff returns during the design and construction stage for both contractor and consultant. It shall further include\nfor all personnel and other costs, disbursements, overheads and profit.\nContract price adjustment shall be applicable in accordance with Clause C2.1.6.\nItem Unit\n3.8.6 Project Liaison Committee (PLC)\n(a) Establishment of PLC lump sum (LS)\n(b) Liaison/meetings with the PLC during Design and provisional sum (PS)\nConstruction Phase\n(c) PLC stipend provisional sum (PS)\n(d) Handling cost i.r.o. item 3.8.6(c) percentage (%)\n(e) Training of PLC members provisional sum (PS)\n(f) Handling cost i.r.o. item 3.8.6(e) percentage (%)\nThe unit of measurement for pay item 3.8.6(a) shall be the lump sum. The sum shall include full compensation\nfor all costs associated with the establishment of the PLC.\nThe provisional sums shall be paid in accordance with Clause C2.1.8.\nThe provisional sum allowed under pay item 3.8.6(b) is to cover all costs associated with liaison/attending\nmeeting with the established PLC during the Design and Construction Phases.\nThe provisional sum allowed under pay item 3.8.6(c) is to cover the monthly payments to PLC members as\nprescribed by the Employer.\nThe rate tendered for handling costs under pay item 3.8.6(d) is for the mark-up or other incidental costs\nincurred by the Service Provider.\nThe provisional sum allowed under pay item 3.8.6(e) is to cover all costs associated with training PLC members\non their duties and responsibilities.\nThe rate tendered under pay item 3.8.6(f) shall include full compensation for all cost associated with producing\na schedule of training requirements, compiling a quotation/tender document, evaluation of quotations or\ntenders received and for the procurement of a training Service Provider on approval by the Employer.\nItem Unit\n3.8.7 Market Analysis and Databases\n(a) Market analysis lump sum (LS)\n(b) Establishment and Maintenance of a Targeted month\nEnterprise database\n(c) Establishment and Maintenance of a Targeted month\nLabour database\nC3-91\n\nThe unit of measurement for item 3.8.7(a) shall be the lump sum.\nThe sum tendered for item 3.8.7(a) shall include full compensation for all costs associated with the market\nanalysis in term of CIDB guidelines for undertaking a feasibility study (CIDB grading, CSD, RRM database,\nlocal/district municipality database, open sessions with contractors from project area, etc.).\nThe units of measurement for items 3.8.7(b) and (c) shall be the month.\nThe sum tendered for item 3.8.7(b) shall include full compensation for all costs associated with the compilation\nand maintenance of a Targeted Enterprise database during the Design and Construction Phase.\nThe sum tendered for item 3.8.7(c) shall include full compensation for all costs associated with the compilation\nand maintenance of a Targeted Labour database during the Construction Phase.\nItem Unit\n3.8.8 Provision of Social Facilitation Services\n(a) Social Facilitator provisional sum (PS)\n(b) Mark-up costs i.t.o. sub-item (a) percentage (%)\nThe provisional sum is to cover the monthly (or pro-rata of a month) Total Annual Cost of Employment (TACE)\nfor the provision of social facilitation service as specified in Clause C3.1.7(k).\nThe provisional sum shall be paid for in accordance with Clause C2.1.8.\nThe unit of measurement for item 3.8.8(b) shall be the percentage. The percentage tendered shall include for\nall costs associated with the sourcing and employment of a Social Facilitator.\nC3-92\n\nC3.9 QUALITY CONTROL: WORKS CONTRACT\nC3.9.1 Materials\na) Scope\nThis section covers the requirements for the provision and quality management of a site laboratory established\nor commercial laboratory to carry out the necessary materials testing and construction quality of the Works.\nb) Standards\nThe Service Provider shall procure a fully operational laboratory and administer the laboratory to undertake\nthe relevant investigative testing as well as process and acceptance control testing in accordance with\nspecified requirements of the Works Contract as well as the Employer's standard requirements. A provisional\nsum has been allowed in the Pricing Schedule for this service. With the release of the SARDS, the Service\nProvider will be required to follow the standard workflow within SARDS with regard to administration of the\nlaboratory.\nThe laboratory shall be a SANAS accredited laboratory or operate under the umbrella of a SANAS accredited\nmain laboratory that shall be responsible for ensuring that all sampling and testing is carried out accurately\nand strictly in accordance with the relevant test methods as well as the SANAS accreditation requirements.\nc) Duties and responsibilities\ni) Establishment and operation of the laboratory\nWhere a site laboratory is required provision for the erection of a laboratory building, together with\nworkbenches, services, furniture etc. will be made under the Works Contract, the planning and\ndocumentation for which shall be included in the tender documentation stage.\nThe Service Provider shall procure the services of a sub-Service Provider for laboratory services\nvia a tender process in accordance with Clause C3.1.12. The successful sub-Service Provider\nshall be SANAS accredited and able to provide laboratory equipment and competent staff for the\noperation of the laboratory. The requirements in terms of the laboratory shall be specified in the\nsub-contract based on the Employer’s proforma document. The appointment of the appropriate\nlaboratory shall be approved by the Employer.\nThe Service Provider shall ensure the supply of such laboratory equipment as necessary to carry\nout the required testing relevant to the scope of the Works. All equipment shall conform strictly to\nSANAS accreditation requirements and/or the specifications as listed in the relevant test methods.\nWith the release of the SARDS, the Service Provider will be required to follow the standard\nworkflow within SARDS with regard to administration of the laboratory.\nd) Testing of materials off-site\nAny testing which may be of such a low frequency as to not warrant the establishment of the relevant equip-\nment and staff on site, or being of a specialised nature, shall be carried out in an off-site SANAS accredited\ncommercial laboratory or the National Reference Laboratory. A provisional sum has been allowed for the costs\nof this testing.\nC3.9.2 Survey\na) Scope\nThis section summarises the requirements for the provision of a site survey service to carry out the necessary\nsurvey quality control on the Works as specified in Technical Methods for Highways TMH11: Standard Survey\nMethods.\nThe purpose of this service is to ensure that the works are constructed as designed and specified and the\nrelevant records are kept up to date. In this regard, the service shall provide for, inter alia:\n• The verification of existing control or the establishment of new control and the supply of the data.\n• The verification of all relevant setting out work carried out by the contractor.\n• The verification that the works are constructed within tolerances specified.\n• The verification of relevant quantities for measurement purposes.\nC3-93\n\n• The checking of designs from a setting out point of view.\n• The verification of construction records (as-built data).\n• To undertake any survey work as required by the Engineer.\nA provisional sum has been allowed in the Pricing Schedule for this service under pay item 3.7.4.\nb) Standards\nThe service shall be undertaken in accordance with TMH11 requirements, COTO Standard Specifications and\nProject Specifications.\nc) Staking of Road Reserve fence positions\nBefore any fence is erected or replaced under the contract, the correct position of the fence must be verified\nby the Employer’s PSP.\nAll requests for verification of the road reserve must be done via the Regional survey representative. Such a\nrequest must include the following information:\n• Route and section\n• Start and end as per blue marker boards\n• Anticipated date when work will commence\n• Contact person on site and his/her contact details\nd) Procurement of the Survey Service\nThe Service Provider shall procure the services of a sub-Service Provider for survey in accordance with Clause\nC3.1.17. A prime cost sum has been allowed in the Pricing Schedule for this service under pay item 3.7.4.\ne) Independent Survey Audits\nAs site supervision survey is a specialised function, the Employer may wish to appoint an independent audit\nsurveyor for each site or the audit surveyor may be assigned a number of sites under his supervision.\nThe audit surveyor shall liaise with the Resident Engineer and conduct monthly audits with the surveyor to:\n• Satisfy the audit team that all the required functions have been attended to.\n• That suitable equipment is used to undertake this function.\n• That the equipment claimed for as part of the quality control function is on site and in good working\ncondition.\n• Check survey procedures and methodology utilised on site.\n• Check the processing of survey data.\n• Check frequency of checks undertaken and the results obtained.\n• Check the efficiency of the data submitted for control purposes.\n• Evaluate the standard of recommendations made to the Resident Engineer with regards to all phases\nof the checked work.\n• Check all records and the back-up of records.\n• Check the signing off of records and the pointing out of non-conformance.\n• Determine how the supervisory surveyor is utilised by the Resident Engineer and whether the Employer\ngets value for the money spent.\n• Provide experiential training specifications, programs and compliance requirements as defined by the\nrelevant academic qualification criteria.\n• Provide registration procedures and programs to ensure that trainees are able to achieve registration\nstatus with the South African Geomatics Council.\n• Attend to training and the implementation of new procedures on site.\n• Check training material and report on the quality of training.\n• Interview trainees and report on progress.\nThe audit surveyor will do a monthly audit report that shall be submitted to the Engineer and the Employer.\nC3-94\n\nC3.9.3 Measurement and payment\nItem Unit\n3.9.1 Laboratory testing\n(a) Provision of a fully operational laboratory prime cost (PC)\n(b) Handling cost i.r.o. sub item 3.9.1(a) percentage (%)\n(c) Off-site materials testing prime cost (PC)\n(d) Handling cost i.r.o. sub item 3.9.1(c) percentage (%)\nThe unit of measurement under sub-items 3.9.1(a) and (c) shall be the prime cost.\nThe prime cost sum shall be paid in accordance with Clause C2.1.8.\nThe percentage (%) tendered under item 3.9.1(b) shall include full compensation associated with the planning,\nscheduling, compilation of tender documentation, issuing of 10 (ten) tenders in electronic and/or paper format\nand evaluation for the procurement of the laboratory service, as well as handling fees.\nThe percentage (%) tendered under item 3.9.1(d) shall include full compensation for procurement of the off-\nsite laboratory testing in terms of the Employer’s requirements, as well as handling fees.\nC3-95\n\nC3.10 CLOSE OUT\nC3.10.1 Scope\nThis section covers the fulfilling and completion of the project close-out including necessary documentation to\nfacilitate effective completion, hand-over and operation of the project.\nThe Service Provider shall administer the Works Contract during the period subsequent to the issuing of the\nTaking-Over Certificate of the Works up to and including the issuing of the Performance Certificate to the\nContractor and conclusion of the final payment certificate.\nC3.10.2 Taking-over Certificate\nUpon a request from the contractor for the Taking-over Certificate (TOC) to be issued in terms of the Conditions\nof Contract the engineer shall establish compliance with the Works Contract for a TOC to be issued.\nThe Service Provider shall undertake a full inspection of the Works (which shall include the health and safety\naspects as far as reasonably practicable) to identify any outstanding minor works, defects and/or damages for\nthe compilation of a snag list. The inspection shall be undertaken by the Engineer and his representative for\nthe Works.\nAfter the inspection and only when the Engineer is of the opinion that a TOC can be issued, the Service\nProvider shall arrange an on-site meeting and further inspection with representatives of the Employer,\nContractor and RRM Service Provider. At this meeting, parties will be presented with the snag list for\nconsideration. There-after an inspection will follow with all the parties involved to add additional items to the\nsnag list.\nAfter the above meeting the engineer shall re-establish compliance with the Works Contract with involvement\nof the Employer for a TOC to be issued, failing which the whole process is to be repeated.\nThe Service Provider shall also complete the updating of all structural inspection (bridges and major culverts)\nand capture into ITIS (using the capture software) and submit to the Employer for approval before the TOC is\nissued.\nThe TOC shall declare the structure (bridges, culverts, roads, sections of roads, etc.) safe for use as required\nin terms of Construction Regulation 6.1(j).\nThe engineer’s representative shall be present on the site during the period required to complete the items on\nthe snag list and monitor the completion thereof, unless otherwise agreed with the Employer.\nC3.10.3 Contractor’s CIDB performance evaluation\nThe Service Provider shall undertake the required performance evaluation of the contractor according to the\nCIDB’s requirements as soon as the contractor requests the issuing of the TOC. The Service Provider shall\nsubmit the above to the Employer at the date of issuing of the TOC.\nC3.10.4 Construction records (As-builts) and Contract report(s)\nThe Service Provider shall prepare and submit, in accordance with the Employer’s standard requirements,\ndraft construction records reflecting the works as constructed as well as any deviations from the designs as\nwell as a draft contract report(s) providing information on how the contract was executed. Once reviewed and\naccepted by the Employer the Service Provider shall prepare and submit the final construction records and\nreport(s).\nC3.10.5 Performance Certificate\nThe Service Provider shall undertake a full inspection of the Works to identify any defects and/or damages\nbefore the end of the defects notification period. The inspections shall be undertaken by the Engineer and his\nrepresentative for the Works. Should both of them not be available, the Service Provider shall propose a person\nhaving sufficient knowledge of assessing the work for approval by the Employer.\nAfter the inspection the Service Provider shall arrange an on-site meeting and further inspection with repre-\nsentatives of the Employer, Contractor and RRM Service Provider. At this meeting, parties will be informed of\nC3-96\n\nthe defects and/or damages identified during the inspection. Thereafter an inspection will follow with all the\nparties involved to identify any further defects and/or damages.\nOnce identified defects and/or damages are addressed the engineer shall notify the Employer that the\nPerformance Certificate can be issued.\nC3.10.6 Co-ordinate and Monitor Remedying of Defects\nThe Service Provider shall co-ordinate and monitor the remedying of any defects and/or damages identified\nduring the defects notification period.\nPrior to the provision of any service under this sub-clause the Service Provider shall submit to the Employer\nfor approval a schedule of personnel necessary to undertake the additional services including a cost estimate.\nPayment for this service shall be made in terms of Clause C.3.8.\nC3.10.7 Final Payment Certificate\nPrior to the compilation of the final payment certificate the engineer shall ensure that all items on the\nEmployer’s checklist are complied with. The signed off checklist shall be submitted with the final payment\ncertificate.\nThe engineer shall only compile and submit the final payment certificate once the Performance Certificate has\nbeen issued by the Employer and all unfulfilled financial obligations have been resolved.\nC3.10.8 Extended Guarantees\nWhere the Service Provider is required to perform services (e.g. inspections after the completion date of the\nWorks Contract) relating to extended guarantees for the Works Contract (e.g. Product Performance\nGuarantees) separate arrangements for remuneration will be made by the Employer under Additional Duties.\nC3-97\n\nC3.10.9 Measurement and Payment\nItem Unit\n3.10.1 Close Out\n(a) Road works:\n(i) Fee (based on Employer’s estimated Cost of the Works) lump sum (LS)\n(ii) Fee adjustment* lump sum (LS)\n(b) Structures (bridges and other major structures):\n(i) Fee (based on Employer’s estimated Cost of the Works) lump sum (LS)\n(ii) Fee adjustment* lump sum (LS)\n(c) Electrical:\n(i) Fee (based on Employer’s estimated Cost of the Works) lump sum (LS)\n(ii) Fee adjustment* lump sum (LS)\n(d) Buildings:\n(i) Fee (based on Employer’s estimated Cost of the Works) lump sum (LS)\n(ii) Fee adjustment* lump sum (LS)\n(e) Mechanical:\n(i) Fee (based on Employer’s estimated Cost of the Works) lump sum (LS)\n(ii) Fee adjustment* lump sum (LS)\n(f) Electronic:\n(i) Fee (based on Employer’s estimated Cost of the Works) lump sum (LS)\n(ii) Fee adjustment* lump sum (LS)\n(g) Other:\n(i) Fee (based on Employer’s estimated Cost of the Works) lump sum (LS)\n(ii) Fee adjustment* lump sum (LS)\n* Note to tenderer: For tender purposes the rate for fee adjustment\nshall be zero. During the contract the applicable rate will be\ncalculated i.t.o. fee adjustment specification.\nGeneral: Method of Payment\nPayment under item 3.10.1 will be made as follows:\nThe unit of measurement shall be the lump sum.\nThe rate under each sub-sub-item (i) shall include full compensation for all duties and requirements associated\nwith the close out stage. It shall further include for all costs, disbursement and profit as well as the transport\nand subsistence costs of the engineer and any personnel required.\nFee adjustments under sub-sub-item (ii) shall only be applicable when the Cost of the Works differs by more\nor less than 20% of the Employer’s estimated Cost of the Works. The fee adjustment shall be determined as\ndescribed in Clause C3.1.4.\nThe Service Provider shall be entitled to render an interim account on submissions of the draft construction\nrecords and draft contract report(s), for up to 50% of the fee for this stage. The adjustment will be based on\nthe Cost of the Works at the time of issuing the TOC. The balance of the fee is claimable upon the submission\nof and approval by the Employer of the final construction records and final contract report(s). The final\nadjustment will be based on the total final Cost of the Works.\nContract price adjustment shall be applicable in accordance with Clause C2.1.6.\nC3-98\n\nPART C4: SITE INFORMATION\nC3-99\n\nTABLE OF CONTENTS PAGE\nC.4.1 LOCATION OF THE PROJECT ...................................................................................... C3-103\nC3-100\n\nC.4.1 LOCATION OF THE PROJECT\nThe proposed project is located at Kloppersbos/Pyramid from (km 0.0) to (km 12.9) under the City of Tshwane\nMetropolitan Municipality.\nC.4.2 GEOMETRIC INFORMATION\nTABLE 4.2\nDESCRIPTION CURRENT ENVISAGED\nProject limits From km 0 to km 12.9 No change\nInterchanges No interchanges No change\nTRH 4 Road Category Gravel Surfaced.\nDesign speed Unknown To be determined\nLevel of service Unknown To be determined\nI/C capacity Not Applicable No change\nCross section . This includes review of the\nnumber, location and type of intersection.\nC.4.3 TRAFFIC INFORMATION\nTraffic information is available in SARDS online.\nC3-101\n\nC.4.4 PAVEMENT INFORMATION\nPavement information is available in SARDS online.\nC.4.5 GEOTECHNICAL\nThe geotechnical investigation is to be conducted to determine the current condition of the road.\nC.4.6 MATERIAL SOURCES\nIdentify material sources required for the maintenance repairs and resurfacing actions.\nProvide information on commercial sources:\nContact details\nTypes of material available\nCompliance of the material with the specifications.\nIdentify potential water sources for construction purposes where/if required.\nOptimise the utilisation of project-identified borrow pits/ hard rock quarries versus commercial\nmaterial sources.\nC.4.7 LAND REQUIREMENTS\nTABLE 4.7\nDESCRIPTION CURRENT ENVISAGED\nPermanent land acquisition Unknown To be determined\nTemporary land requirements Unknown To be determined\nC.4.8 ENVIRONMENTAL REQUIREMENTS\nTABLE 4.8\nDESCRIPTION CURRENT ENVISAGED\nEIA/EMP Remaining within existing road reserve To be determined.\nC.4.9 DRAINAGE\nDrainage forms an integral part of the rehabilitation and upgrade design. Initial assessments indicate that the\nexisting side drains have insufficient capacity, which may be exacerbated by the addition of additional lanes.\nIt is also assumed that the existing sub surface drains are not fully functional in areas and will thus require\nreplacement. There are a number of pipe culverts present along the route which appear to be of corrugated\nmetal type. These will require relining or replacing, which may involve pipe jacking. A new median drain system\nwill have to be designed to cater for the additional lanes added in the median.\nTABLE 4.9\nDESCRIPTION CURRENT ENVISAGED\nSide drains None New level and increased capacity\ndepending on rehab strategy\nMedian drains None New median drains to accommodate\nadditional lanes\nMeadow drains None Concrete linings to be constructed\nSub-surface drains None To be tested, assessed and probably\nreplaced\nC3-102\n\nPipe culverts None To be assessed and relined or replaced\nC.4.10 ROAD FURNITURE\nTABLE 4.10\nDESCRIPTION CURRENT ENVISAGED\nRoad Signs & Route marker None To be determined\nboards\nGuard rails None To be determined.\nMedian guard rails None To be determined.\nRoad studs None To be installed on the new surfacing.\nC.4.11 STRUCTURES\nStructures information is available in SARDS online.\nA pre-populated Structures Tracking Spreadsheet is provided in Appendix N. After award, the consultant is responsible\nfor continuously updating this spreadsheet and submitting to the SANRAL Project Manager as the project\nprogresses.\nC.4.12 SUB-SERVICE PROVIDERS\nThe following sub-service will be required on this project: (Survey\n• Environmental Assessment Practitioner (EAP) and Environmental Compliance Officer (ECO)\n• External OHS auditor\n• Specialist technical sub-services\n• Site laboratory\nC.4.13 PRICING AND PAYMENT\nThe Service Provider, when pricing the fee, he intends to receive for the provision of the services, should take\naccount of the ECSA fee scale guidelines provided in Appendix E. The fee offered should be exclusive of costs\nto conduct visual condition surveys and produce construction tender documentation Adjustments to the fee for\nvariations that result in higher project construction cost than the estimate provided in Part C.3.1.4: Project Cost\nEstimate, may be considered according to the formula given in the scope of works.\nC.4.14 APPENDICES\nAppendix A: Locality Plan\nAppendix B: Traffic Information\nAppendix C: Pavement Information\nAppendix D: Occupational Health & Safety\nAppendix E: ECSA fee scale guideline\nAppendix F: Proforma report formats\nAppendix G: Request for Survey work\nAppendix H: Memorandums of Agreement\nC3-103\n\nAppendix I: Road Safety Audit Policy\nAppendix J: 2nd Tier Procurement Procedures\nAppendix K: Integrated Transport Information System\nAppendix L: Gateway Review Template\nAppendix M: SARDS Manual\nAppendix N: Structures Tracking spreadsheet\nC3-104\n\nAppendix A: Locality Plan\nAvailable in SARDS online\nC3-105\n\nAppendix B: Traffic Information\nAvailable in SARDS online\nC3-106\n\nAppendix C: Pavement Information\nAvailable in SARDS online\nC3-107\n\nAppendix D: Occupational Health & Safety\nD1 : Health and Safety Specification for Service Providers during Construction\nD2 : Health and Safety Specification for Contractors during Construction\nD3 : OHS Audit Questionnaire\nC3-108\n\nD1: Health and Safety specification for Service Provider during Construction\nTHE SOUTH AFRICAN NATIONAL\nROADS AGENCY SOC LIMITED\nOCCUPATIONAL HEALTH AND SAFETY SPECIFICATIONS for\nSERVICE PROVIDER\nCONTRACT SANRAL C.002-056-2024/1\nSCOPE OF WORKS: CD PROJECT FOR R573-010-2023/1\nUPGRADE OF KLOPPERSBOS/PYRAMID ROAD FROM\nGRAVEL TO SURFACED.\nC3-109\n\nTABLE OF CONTENTS PAGE\n1.
